## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can track down and fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 하나 꼽자면, 깨진 테스트나 빌드 오류를 찾아서 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは届いた順序どおり確実に届けるが遅れやすく、UDPは確実性や順序の保証を省いて速さを優先します。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, sobre todo si nadie más trabaja en ella. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otras personas usan, porque reescribir su historia con rebase les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接的，保证数据可靠、有序送达，代价是更多的握手和确认开销；UDP 不做这些保证，但更轻、延迟更低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is your own and nobody else has pulled it yet, to tidy its commits onto the latest base for a linear history. Merge when others are working on the branch or it's already pushed, since rewriting shared history causes trouble.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接指定文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

只写文件名中的一部分也可以，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果还想只跑文件里的某一个用例，加 `-t`：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "用例名"
```

- 全量测试通过，把测试输出和 diff 摘要报给我。

不做的事：不要推送，不要开 PR，不要合并到主分支。改好先给我看。

地点：~/dev/hawdb，分支 ducter/compaction-l0。
  [retitled task compaction] hawdb: compaction 按 L0 文件数触发，阈值可配置（默认 8）
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records every change before applying it to the main data files. If the system crashes mid-write, the database replays the log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.
```

  [partner message] 好了，夜间写入的数据已经填进去了：第 2、3 晚的结果都在上面，之后每晚的报告会自动更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2269 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second, p99 latency, compaction stalls</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">The first night's report will show up here.</div>
</div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-none { color: var(--d-muted); }
</style>
<script>
  function fmt(n) { return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : String(n); }
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    var latest = document.getElementById('latest');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
    list.textContent = '';
    if (!nights || !nights.length) {
      empty.style.display = '';
      latest.textContent = '';
      latest.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">Not yet</span>';
      ducter.summary('No nights yet');
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    var rows = nights.slice().reverse();
    rows.forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.night;
      var s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'k-row-sub';
      s.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + n.stalls;
      main.appendChild(s);
      var v = document.createElement('div');
      v.className = 'k-row-value';
      v.textContent = fmt(n.writes) + ' writes/s';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(v);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls + ' · night ' + last.night;
    ducter.summary(fmt(last.writes) + '/s, p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms');
  }
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
  });
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

})();
$('days').onclick = ev => {
  const rm = ev.target.closest('[data-rm]');
  if (rm) { save(list().filter(e => e.id !== rm.dataset.rm)); sel = null; render(); return; }
  const row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (row) { sel = sel === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id; render(); }
};
$('add').onclick = () => {
  const day = $('day').value.trim();
  const item = $('item').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!day || !item || !(amount >= 0)) return;
  save([...list(), { id: Date.now() + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day, item, amount }]);
  $('item').value = '';
  $('amt').value = '';
  $('item').focus();
  render();
};
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记，点下面的「记一笔」</div>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-26"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<style>
.k-sum-value{font-size:44px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-row-value{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
</style>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN",{maximumFractionDigits:2});}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let total=0;
  for(const x of xs){
    total+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(sub);
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length? xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden=xs.length>0;
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total) : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").addEventListener("click",function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||"2026-11-26";
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
});
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记一笔。点下面的「记一笔」加上第一笔。</div>
</div>

<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><div class="k-chips" id="cats"></div></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>

<div class="k-chips">

<button type="button" class="k-btn" id="cancel" style="display:none">取消</button>
</div>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const KEY="items";
const CATS=["交通","住宿","餐饮","其他"];
let xs=[], cat="交通" … set(){
  ed=-1;
  ["trip","day","amt","note"].forEach(id=>$(id).value="");
  cat="交通"; drawCats();
  $("save").textContent="保存";
  $("del").style.display="none"; $("cancel").style.display="none";
  $("addBox").open=false;
}
$("list").onclick=e=>{
  const r=e.target.closest("[data-i]"); if(!r)return;
  ed=+r.dataset.i; const x=xs[ed];
  $("trip").value=x.trip||""; $("day").value=x.day||""; $("amt").value=x.amt||""; $("note").value=x.note||"";
  cat=x.cat||"交通"; drawCats();
  $("save").textContent="保存修改";
  $("del").style.display=""; $("cancel").style.display="";
  $("addBox").open=true;
};
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt) return;
  const old = ed>=0 ? xs[ed] : {};
  const item = Object.assign({}, old, {trip:$("trip").value.trim(), day:$("day").value.trim(), cat:cat, amt:amt, note:$("note").value.trim()});
  if(ed>=0) xs[ed]=item; else xs.push(item);
  ducter.set(KEY, xs); reset(); render();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{ if(ed<0)return; xs.splice(ed,1); ducter.set(KEY, xs); reset(); render(); };
$("cancel").onclick=()=>{ reset(); };
ducter.onChange(()=>{ xs=ducter.get(KEY)||[]; render(); });
xs=ducter.get(KEY)||[];
drawCats(); render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南","from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚 11/20–21","from":"你的预订"}]}
